# Commands · T · part 2

1,566 entries, task-init to tdd-red. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **task-init**: Initialize the task folder and base task memory inside projects/&lt;client&gt;__&lt;project&gt;/active/YYYY-MM-DD_&lt;task-slug&gt;/, seeded from PROJECT_CHARTER.md when present, or from a vetted issue-tracker MCP item (gated, off by default). Use at the START of any engineering work in this workflow's repositories, before the first file is edited, including a one-line docs change or a typo fix (small scope never skips the workflow). Do not use when the task folder exists, to resume (use resume-from-state), or to update memory (use sync-task-state). For a brief with 3 or more independent sub-tasks, use task-in… ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/task-init.md) · ★10)

- **task-init-fleet**: Orchestrator-workers variant of task-init that decomposes a complex brief into N parallel independent sub-tasks, each worker running a full task-init. Use when the brief contains N &gt;= 3 logically independent work streams (e.g., REST-&gt;GraphQL migration across api-server + mobile-app + web-app; or implement-X for 4 distinct features that share infra but not delivery). Do not use for one cohesive task (use task-init), for sequential dependencies that cannot run in parallel, or when sub-task scopes are unclear (run decision-interview first). ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/task-init-fleet.md) · ★10)

For tests on EXISTING code without changing behavior, use the `tester` skill instead.
